import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/b4_helpers.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_network.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/helpers.dart';

/// 持久化 I/O 竞态，所有用户等待可取消。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanCachePersist.read] 慢读取被 clear remove dispose 作废，旧读取不回填',
      () async {
    for (final action in ['clear', 'remove', 'dispose']) {
      final store = B4Persist()
        ..snapshot = true
        ..readGate = Completer<void>()
        ..values[b4Key] = {'data': 'old', 'expiresAt': 60000};
      final net = FakeNetwork();
      final api = testClient(network: net, plugins: [
        DiomanCache<Result>(persist: store, cachePolicy: DiomanCachePolicy.both)
      ]);
      final cache = api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!;
      final pending =
          api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
      await b4Turn();
      if (action == 'clear') {
        await cache.clear();
      } else if (action == 'remove') {
        await cache.remove(b4Key);
      } else {
        api.eject(DiomanCache.pluginName);
      }
      store.readGate!.complete();
      expect((await pending).data, isNot('old'));
      expect(cache.entryCount, 0);
      expect(cache.pendingWrites, 0);
    }
  });
  test('[DiomanCachePersist.write] clear 等待在途持久化写入后 erase，错误原样且尾链可恢复',
      () async {
    final store = B4Persist()..writeGate = Completer<void>();
    final api = testClient(plugins: [
      DiomanCache<Result>(persist: store, cachePolicy: DiomanCachePolicy.both)
    ]);
    final cache = api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!;
    final pending = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
    await store.writeStarted.future;
    var cleared = false;
    final clear = cache.clear().then((_) => cleared = true);
    await b4Turn();
    expect(cleared, isFalse);
    store.writeGate!.complete();
    await pending;
    await clear;
    expect(store.values, isEmpty);
    expect(cache.entryCount, 0);
    expect(cache.pendingWrites, 0);
    for (final action in ['read', 'write', 'remove', 'erase']) {
      final p = B4Persist()..error = StateError('persist');
      final c = testClient(plugins: [
        DiomanCache<Result>(persist: p, cachePolicy: DiomanCachePolicy.persist)
      ]);
      if (action == 'write') {
        p.error = null;
        p.writeGate = Completer<void>();
        final future =
            c.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
        await b4Turn();
        p.error = StateError('write');
        final check = expectLater(future, throwsStateError);
        p.writeGate!.complete();
        await check;
      } else if (action == 'read') {
        await expectLater(
            c.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true)),
            throwsStateError);
      } else if (action == 'remove') {
        await expectLater(
            c.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.remove(b4Key), throwsStateError);
      } else {
        await expectLater(
            c.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.clear(), throwsStateError);
      }
      p.error = null;
      await c.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.clear();
      expect(c.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.pendingWrites, 0);
    }
  });
  test('[DiomanCachePersist.read] 旧过期慢读取不得删除较新写入，持久命中按容量提升', () async {
    final store = B4Persist()
      ..snapshot = true
      ..readGate = Completer<void>()
      ..values[b4Key] = {'data': 'old', 'expiresAt': -1};
    final net = FakeNetwork();
    final api = testClient(network: net, plugins: [
      DiomanCache<Result>(
          persist: store, cachePolicy: DiomanCachePolicy.both, maxEntries: 1)
    ]);
    final old = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
    await b4Turn();
    final gate = store.readGate!;
    store.readGate = null;
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
    final saved = store.values[b4Key];
    gate.complete();
    await old;
    expect(store.values[b4Key], saved);
    expect(api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.pendingWrites, 0);
    for (final key in ['a', 'b']) {
      store.values[key] = {'data': key, 'expiresAt': 60000};
    }
    for (final key in ['a', 'b', 'a']) {
      expect(
          (await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path,
                  call: b4Call(cache: true, key: key)))
              .data,
          key);
      expect(api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.entryCount, 1);
    }
  });
  test('[DiomanCachePersist.read × DiomanContext.wait] 取消读取、过期清理、尾链、写入均清理订阅',
      () async {
    for (final phase in ['read', 'tail', 'remove', 'write']) {
      final store = B4Persist();
      final api = testClient(plugins: [
        DiomanCache<Result>(
            persist: store, cachePolicy: DiomanCachePolicy.persist)
      ]);
      final cache = api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!;
      if (phase == 'read') {
        store.readGate = Completer<void>();
      }
      if (phase == 'write' || phase == 'tail') {
        store.writeGate = Completer<void>();
      }
      Future<DiomanResponse<Object?>>? first;
      if (phase == 'tail') {
        first = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
        await b4Turn();
      }
      final pending =
          api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
      await b4Turn();
      api.cancelAll();
      expect((await pending).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
      store.readGate?.complete();
      store.writeGate?.complete();
      if (first != null) {
        expect((await first).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
      }
      await b4Turn();
      expect(cache.pendingWrites, 0);
      expect(api.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
    }
  });
  test('[DiomanCache.clone] 写入快照隔离来源和返回值，扩展工厂保留标记', () async {
    final body = <int, dynamic>{
      1: <int>[2]
    };
    final source = TestPlugin('source',
        stage: DiomanStage.source,
        handler: (ctx, next) async =>
            ctx.fail(DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.ok, data: body)));
    final api = testClient(plugins: [
      const DiomanCache<Result>(clone: DiomanClonePolicy.deep),
      source
    ]);
    final first =
        await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
    (body[1] as List)[0] = 9;
    (first.data as Map)[1][0] = 7;
    expect(
        (await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true)))
            .data,
        {
          1: [2]
        });
  });
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/b4_helpers.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_clock.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_network.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/helpers.dart';

/// 四个 source 插件的公开规则。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanMock.routes] 默认生效，内联数据经工厂识别 business 且零触网', () async {
    final net = FakeNetwork();
    final api = testClient(network: net, plugins: [
      DiomanMock<Result>(routes: {
        b4Route: (_) => {'code': 401, 'data': null}
      })
    ]);
    expect((await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path)).kind, DiomanKind.biz);
    expect(net.sends, isEmpty);
    expect(
        (await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(mock: false))).ok,
        isTrue);
    expect(net.sends.length, 1);
  });
  test('[DiomanMock.mockUrl] 404 与 network 默认回落，500 保留，缺地址 debug 告警', () async {
    for (final status in [404, 500]) {
      final net = FakeNetwork()
        ..routes['GET:https://mock.test$b4Path'] = [FakeReply(status: status)];
      final api = testClient(
          network: net,
          plugins: [const DiomanMock<Result>(mockUrl: 'https://mock.test/')]);
      final r = await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
      expect(r.httpStatus, status == 404 ? 200 : 500);
      expect(net.sends.length, status == 404 ? 2 : 1);
    }
    final logs = <String>[];
    final api =
        testClient(debug: logs.add, plugins: [const DiomanMock<Result>()]);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    expect(logs.join(), contains('mockUrl'));
  });
  test('[DiomanShare.policy] start 只发一次，跟随者为相同只读 R，省略不开启', () async {
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    final net = FakeNetwork()..routes[b4Route] = [FakeReply(gate: gate.future)];
    final api =
        testClient(network: net, plugins: [const DiomanShare<Result>()]);
    final a = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(share: true));
    final b = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(share: true));
    await b4Turn();
    expect(net.sends.length, 1);
    gate.complete();
    final results = await Future.wait([a, b]);
    expect(identical(results[0], results[1]), isTrue);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    expect(net.sends.length, 3);
  });
  test('[DiomanShare.policy] end 最新失败归所有，race 首成功与全失败最后结果', () async {
    for (final policy in [DiomanSharePolicy.end, DiomanSharePolicy.race]) {
      for (final successful in [false, true]) {
        final first = Completer<void>();
        final second = Completer<void>();
        final net = FakeNetwork()
          ..routes[b4Route] = [
            FakeReply(status: 500, gate: first.future),
            FakeReply(status: successful ? 200 : 503, gate: second.future)
          ];
        final api =
            testClient(network: net, plugins: [const DiomanShare<Result>()]);
        final a = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(share: policy));
        final b = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(share: policy));
        await b4Turn();
        first.complete();
        await b4Turn();
        second.complete();
        final r = await Future.wait([a, b]);
        expect(
            r.map((r) => r.httpStatus), everyElement(successful ? 200 : 503));
        expect(identical(r[0], r[1]), isTrue);
      }
    }
  });
  test('[DiomanShare.retries] 内部 retry 只暴露最终结果，间隔计时可清理', () async {
    final clock = FakeClock();
    final net = FakeNetwork()
      ..routes[b4Route] = [const FakeReply(status: 500), const FakeReply()];
    final api = testClient(network: net, clock: clock, plugins: [
      const DiomanShare<Result>(
          policy: DiomanSharePolicy.retry, interval: b4Tick)
    ]);
    final a = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(share: true));
    final b = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(share: true));
    await b4Turn();
    expect(clock.pendingTimers, 1);
    clock.elapse(b4Tick);
    expect((await a).ok, isTrue);
    expect((await b).ok, isTrue);
    expect(net.sends.length, 2);
    expect(clock.pendingTimers, 0);
  });
  test(
      '[DiomanCache.option] true 继承 clone expires shouldCache，Duration 与覆盖选项生效',
      () async {
    final net = FakeNetwork();
    final clock = FakeClock();
    var clones = 0;
    final api = testClient(network: net, clock: clock, plugins: [
      DiomanCache<Result>(
          expires: b4Tick,
          clone: (Object? data) {
            clones++;
            return data;
          },
          shouldCache: (_) => true)
    ]);
    final call = b4Call(cache: true);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: call);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: call);
    expect(net.sends.length, 1);
    expect(clones, 3);
    clock.elapse(b4Tick);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path,
        call: b4Call(cache: const Duration(seconds: 2)));
    clock.elapse(b4Tick);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: call);
    expect(net.sends.length, 2);
    await api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.remove(b4Key);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path,
        call: b4Call(
            cache: const DiomanCacheOptions(clone: DiomanClonePolicy.none)));
    expect(clones, 6);
    expect(net.sends.length, 3);
    await api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.clear();
    expect(api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.entryCount, 0);
  });
  test('[DiomanCache.shouldStore] business 失败不存储且不经过用户 clone，GET HEAD 默认允许',
      () async {
    final net = FakeNetwork()
      ..routes[b4Route] = [
        const FakeReply(body: {'code': 500, 'data': null})
      ];
    final api = testClient(network: net, plugins: [
      DiomanCache<Result>(clone: (Object? data) => throw StateError('不该克隆'))
    ]);
    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      expect(
          (await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true)))
              .kind,
          DiomanKind.biz);
    }
    expect(net.sends.length, 2);
    expect(api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.pendingWrites, 0);
    final head = testClient(
        network: net,
        plugins: [const DiomanCache<Result>(clone: DiomanClonePolicy.none)]);
    await head.send<Object?>('HEAD', b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
    await head.send<Object?>('HEAD', b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
    expect(net.sends.length, 3);
  });
  test('[DiomanCache.cachePolicy] memo persist both 分层，持久化命中提升且惰性过期', () async {
    for (final policy in DiomanCachePolicy.values) {
      final store = B4Persist();
      final clock = FakeClock();
      final net = FakeNetwork();
      final api = testClient(network: net, clock: clock, plugins: [
        DiomanCache<Result>(
            persist: store, cachePolicy: policy, expires: b4Tick)
      ]);
      await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
      await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
      expect(net.sends.length, 1);
      expect(store.writes, policy == DiomanCachePolicy.memo ? 0 : 1);
      clock.elapse(b4Tick);
      await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path, call: b4Call(cache: true));
      expect(net.sends.length, 2);
      await api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.remove(b4Key);
      await api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.clear();
      expect(store.values, isEmpty);
      expect(api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()!.pendingWrites, 0);
    }
  });
  test('[DiomanOfflineApi.flush] 离线排队恢复和手动 flush，shouldQueue false 放行',
      () async {
    final stream = StreamController<bool>.broadcast(sync: true);
    addTearDown(stream.close);
    var online = false;
    final net = FakeNetwork();
    final api = testClient(network: net, plugins: [
      DiomanOffline<Result>(
          isOnline: () => online, onConnectivityChanged: stream.stream)
    ]);
    final a = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    await b4Turn();
    expect(api.plugin<DiomanOfflineApi>()!.pending, 1);
    expect(net.sends, isEmpty);
    stream.add(false);
    await b4Turn();
    expect(net.sends, isEmpty);
    online = true;
    stream.add(true);
    expect((await a).ok, isTrue);
    online = false;
    final b = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    await b4Turn();
    api.plugin<DiomanOfflineApi>()!.flush();
    expect((await b).ok, isTrue);
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path,
        call: b4Call(offline: DiomanOfflineOptions(shouldQueue: (_) => false)));
    online = true;
    await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    expect(net.sends.length, 4);
    api.dispose();
  });
}

---
title: dioman 内置插件与内核服务规格（洋葱管线）
status: active
updated: 2026-10-29
summary: 12 个内置插件和内核服务在 next() 模型下的选项、默认值、行为、内核依赖，以及相对 0.7 的行为变化汇总
---
## TL;DR

- 12 个内置插件：outer 阶段 loading、notify；prepare 阶段 repath、filter、timeout；attempt 阶段 retry、breaker；credential 阶段 auth；source 阶段 mock、share、cache、offline。envs 只有 `setup`，不进链。
- key、cancel、normalize、log 降为内核能力（见[核心架构](pipeline-architecture.md)）。
- 现有功能全部保留，只改写法；行为变化集中列在 §9，CHANGELOG 和迁移指南按它写。

## 0. 插件约定

1. 工厂即类：`DiomanRetry(…)` 等，`name` 为 `dioman:<名字>`。插件对象只放配置，可以是 const；状态建在 `setup` 返回的实例里。
2. **插件级 `enabled: false`**：内核在编译链时直接跳过，不调 `setup`，`inspect()` 不列出。
3. **请求级选项统一四种写法**，由 `resolveOption` 一个函数解析：省略、`true`（用插件级配置）、`false`（本次关闭）、插件自己的选项对象（字段为 null 的沿用插件级）。部分插件另有一种简写（retry 的整数 = `max`，cache 的 `Duration` = `expires`，share 的策略枚举）。不认识的类型按 bug 抛 `ArgumentError`。插件级配置在 `setup` 时预合并；请求级 `true` 或省略时复用同一份。
4. **装上即生效**，只有改变数据新鲜度的 **cache、share 要在请求级开启**（optIn）。
5. **改请求只用 `next(patch)`**，不改 `ctx.request`。
6. 用户回调抛错：通知类隔离并输出错误日志；判定类按 bug 处理；`onRefresh` 抛错算刷新失败（见核心架构 §7）。
7. **每个 await 之后用 `ctx.cancelled()` 重查取消**；等待一律用 `ctx.wait`。
8. 会重发请求的插件（retry、auth、mock、share 的 retry 策略）在第一次 `next()` 之前调用 `ctx.markReplayable()`。

## 1. outer

### loading
- 选项：`onChanged(bool visible)` 必填；`delay`（0→1 之后延迟显示，期间回落就不显示）、`delayClose`（1→0 之后延迟隐藏，期间有新请求就不隐藏），默认都是 `Duration.zero`。请求级：`false`、`true`、`DiomanLoadingOptions`。
- 模型：计数加一；`try { return await next(); } finally { 计数减一 }`。一个逻辑请求恰好一对，重试和重放都包在里面。按回调身份分开计数：请求级换了回调的请求，计入自己那个回调。
- `dispose`：取消未触发的计时器；对已显示的回调立即发一次 `false`；在途请求不再通知。
- 内核依赖：outer 阶段位置、`DiomanClock`。

### notify
- 选项：`notify(String message, R r)` 必填；`stringify(R r, DiomanContext ctx)`，默认在 `r.ok`、`kind == cancel`、`ctx.request.silent` 时返回空串，否则返回 `r.message`，为空时返回 `'<kind> <status>'`。空串表示不通知。请求级只有开关。
- 模型：`final r = await next(); 通知; return r;`。每个逻辑请求最多一次，天然只在最终失败时通知，刷新成功不通知。
- 内核依赖：outer 阶段、`request.silent`。

## 2. prepare

### repath
- 选项不变：`pattern`（默认匹配 `{id}`、`:id`、`[id]`）、`removeKey`（默认 true）。只处理路径部分，先找 query（含 BaseOptions 的 queryParameters）再找 Map 形式的 data；值按路径段编码；同名占位符多次出现全部替换。算出新的 path/query/data 后 `next(patch)`；没有变化直接 `next()`。

### filter
- 选项：`predicate(key, value)`，默认剔除 null、NaN、空白字符串，保留 0 与 false；`ignoreKeys`、`ignoreValues`。剥 query 和 Map 形式的 data 的顶层字段；FormData、Stream 原样。没有可剔除的字段时不打补丁。
- 变化：**装上即生效**（0.7 也是），默认谓词新增剔除 NaN（testkit 中立语义，且 `jsonEncode(double.nan)` 会抛错）。

  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:33:- 选项：`notify(String message, R r)` 必填；`stringify(R r, DiomanContext ctx)

'`。空串表示不通知。请求级只有开关。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:34:- 模型：`final r = await next(); 通知; return r;`。每个逻辑请求最多一次，天然
只在最终失败时通知，刷新成功不通知。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:35:- 内核依赖：outer 阶段、`request.silent`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:36:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:37:## 2. prepare
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:38:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:39:### repath
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:40:- 选项不变：`pattern`（默认匹配 `{id}`、`:id`、`[id]`）、`removeKey`（默认 tru
e）。只处理路径部分，先找 query（含 BaseOptions 的 queryParameters）再找 Map 形式的 data；值按路径段编码；同名占位符多
次出现全部替换。算出新的 path/query/data 后 `next(patch)`；没有变化直接 `next()`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:41:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:42:### filter
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:43:- 选项：`predicate(key, value)`，默认剔除 null、NaN、空白字符串，保留 0 与 fal
se；`ignoreKeys`、`ignoreValues`。剥 query 和 Map 形式的 data 的顶层字段；FormData、Stream 原样。没有可剔除的字段时不打
补丁。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:44:- 变化：**装上即生效**（0.7 也是），默认谓词新增剔除 NaN（testkit 中立语义，且
 `jsonEncode(double.nan)` 会抛错）。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:45:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:46:### timeout
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:47:- 选项不变：`timeouts`（按 `NetworkQuality` 档位的 connect/receive/send）、`pr
obe`（返回当前网络档位）。请求级：`false`、`DiomanTimeoutOptions`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:48:- 模型：读出本次档位对应的超时，`next(patch)` 写入请求的超时字段。只改配置，真
正计时由 Dio 执行（假设 H5，已在本地回环核对）。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:49:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:50:## 3. attempt
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:51:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:52:### retry
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:53:- 选项沿用 0.7：`max`、`methods`（硬否决）、`statusCodes`（默认 408/429/500/50
2/503/504，读**逻辑状态** `r.status`）、`shouldRetry(R r, int attempt)`、`delay`、`jitter`、`delayMax`、`respectRetryAf
ter`（默认 true，命中 `afterStatusCodes` 默认 413/429/503 时按 `Retry-After` 的秒数或 HTTP 日期等待，上限 `retryAfterMa
x`，不叠 jitter、不受 delayMax 限制）、`onRetry(attempt)`。请求级：整数 = max、`false`、`true`、`DiomanRetryOptions`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:54:- 判定顺序：请求级关闭 → 方法不在白名单 → `kind` 属于 cancel、auth、internal、
parse、breaker、offline 时不重试 → `shouldRetry` 返回布尔就采用 → network、timeout 重试 → `statusCodes` 包含 `r.status`
 时重试。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:55:- 模型：`markReplayable()`；`var r = await next(); for (attempt = 1..max) { 判
定; 等待 ctx.wait(delay); 取消则返回; r = await next(); } return r;`。次数用完返回最后一次结果，由落定决定抛不抛。每次
尝试都会穿过 breaker、auth 和 source 阶段。
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:56:- 删除：重放交接、认领判断（`_notAdmitted`）、`share`/`cancel` 兼容 setter、`b
reaker` 字段。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:57:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:58:### breaker
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:59:- 选项沿用 0.7：`failureThreshold`、`resetDuration`、`halfOpenMaxCalls`、`shou
ldTrip(R r)`（默认：`kind` 是 network/timeout，或逻辑状态 ≥500 或 429 时计失败；cancel、internal 不计）、`keyBuilder(re
quest)`（默认 `METHOD:path`）、`onStateChange`、`idleTtl`、`maxBuckets`、`clock`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:60:- 模型：先申请放行；熔断打开或半开名额已满时，直接返回 `ctx.fail(kind: breaker
, reason: 'open')`。放行后 `r = await next()`；按 `shouldTrip` 记一次成或败；cancel 只归还名额、不计成败。在 retry 之内
，所以每次尝试都会计数；熔断打开时 retry 遇到 `kind: breaker` 不再重试，不需要接线。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:61:- 删除：`admit/recordResponse/recordError` 公开方法（原本只为 retry 接线而存在
）、`DiomanBreakerOpenException`（改为 `kind: breaker`）。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:62:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:63:## 4. credential：auth
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:64:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:65:- 选项沿用 0.7：`tokenManager`、`onRefresh`、`onAccessExpired` 必填；`onAccess
Denied`（缺省回退 expired）；`onFailure(R r, tokenManager)`，默认见下；`isProtected(request)`；`ready(tokenManager, req
uest)` 返回 `RequestPatch`，默认写 `Authorization: Bearer <token>`（`headerKey`、`buildHeader` 保留为便捷写法）；`expir
esAt`、`refreshLeeway`、`clock` 用于主动刷新。请求级：`false`、`DiomanAuthOptions`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:66:- 默认失败判定（读逻辑状态，所以 business 风格里的业务 401 自动适用）：`r.stat
us == 401` 时，本地没有 token → expired；**本次注入的 token 不等于当前 token → replay**（说明已被并发刷新）；否则 refre
sh。`403` → deny；其余 → others。本次注入的 token 记在状态键里，不去解析请求头。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:67:- 模型：
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:68:  1. 不受保护，直接 `return next()`；`markReplayable()`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:69:  2. 如果正在刷新，`ctx.wait` 等刷新结果；取消就返回；刷新失败返回 `ctx.fail(k
ind: auth, reason: 'expired')`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:70:  3. 循环：本地没有 token → 通知 denied，返回 `reason: 'unauthenticated'`；即
将过期且配了 `expiresAt` → 先主动刷新；注入 token 并 `r = await next(patch)`；ok、internal、cancel 直接返回；`action = 
await onFailure(r)`，回来**先查取消**；refresh/replay：已经重放过一次就按 expired 处理（防止循环），否则 refresh 时加入
或发起单飞刷新，然后再循环一次；deny → 通知并返回 `reason: 'denied'`；expired → 清会话、通知、返回 `reason: 'expired'`
；others → 原样返回。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:71:- **会话判定**沿用 2026-10-28 的修复：按失败请求实际携带的 token 判断属于哪个
会话，旧请求的失败不清新会话，每个会话只通知一次过期；单飞刷新以 `Future(() => onRefresh(…))` 强制异步发起，同步抛错也
不会卡死刷新窗口。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:72:- **重放前重查本地凭证**：刷新成功后、重放之前使用者登出，就不再重放（axp B3 
抓到的缺陷）。
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:73:- 删除：重放交接、认领结算、`DiomanAuth.dio`/`share` setter。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:74:
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:75:## 5. source：mock → share → cache → offline
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:76:
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:77:### mock
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:78:- 选项：`enabled`（默认 true，装上即生效）、`routes`（`METHOD:path` → 处理函数
，内联 mock）、`mockUrl`（转发到 mock 服务）、`fallbackWhen(R r)`，默认 `r.httpStatus == 404 || r.kind == network`。请
求级：`false`、`true`、`DiomanMockOptions`。
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:79:- 模型：命中内联路由 → 用处理函数的结果经工厂造 `R`，直接返回。配了 `mockUrl` 
→ `markReplayable()`，`probe = await next(patch: 改写到 mockUrl，并带"不走 cache、不参与 share"的状态标记)`；`fallbackW
hen(probe)` 为真时再 `next()` 打真实接口。
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:80:- 变化：从传输层 adapter（2026-10-28）回到 source 阶段，`DiomanMockAdapter` 删
除，cache 的 `x-dioman-mock` 规则删除。具备 testkit 的 `mock-in-chain` 能力。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:81:
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:82:### share
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:83:- 选项：`policy`（start、end、race、retry，默认 start）、`retries`、`interval`
（retry 策略用）、`methods`（默认 GET、HEAD）。请求级（optIn）：`true`、策略枚举、`DiomanShareOptions`、`false`。`ctx.k
ey()` 为 null 时直接放行。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:84:- 模型：
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:85:  - start：首发 `next(patch: cancelToken = 共享 token，并去掉 Dio 层超时)`；每
个订阅者用 `ctx.wait(共享结果, timeout: 自己的超时)`，各自离开只结束自己；最后一个订阅者离开时才取消共享请求。首发取消
不连累跟随者。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:86:  - end：每次都发，只有序号最新的结果落定。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:87:  - race：每次都发，第一个 `r.ok` 的结果胜出，全部失败时用最后一个。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:88:  - retry：共享同一个结果，失败时在内部按 `retries`、`interval` 重试，调用方只
看到最终一次。
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:89:- 跟随者拿到同一个只读的 `R`。共享以"一次尝试"为单位；retry 在外层，每次重试都
会重新进入 share。共享表按策略分开存放。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:90:- 删除：结算登记簿、`hasMultipleDownstreamSettlers`、`registerDownstreamSettle
r`、`isJoined`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:91:
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:92:### cache
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:93:- 选项沿用 0.7：`persist`（存储接口）、`cachePolicy`（memo、persist、both）、`
expires`、`clone`、`maxEntries`、`shouldCache(request)`（默认 GET、HEAD）、`shouldStore(R r)`（默认 `r.ok`）、`clock`。
请求级（optIn）：`true`、`Duration` = expires、`DiomanCacheOptions`、`false`。
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:94:- 模型：方法不允许、key 为 null、或带 mock 探测标记时直接放行；命中（惰性检查
过期）直接返回，不触网；未命中时登记新鲜度屏障，`r = await next()`，屏障允许且 `shouldStore(r)` 才写入，`finally` 回收
屏障记录。管理操作 `api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()` 提供 `remove`、`clear`；`clear()` 会作废在途的写入。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:95:- 变化：`shouldStore` 默认就是 `r.ok`（归一化之后业务失败天然不会进缓存），不
再需要和 normalize 接线。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:96:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:97:### offline
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:98:- 选项沿用 0.7：`isOnline()`、`onConnectivityChanged`、`shouldQueue(request)`
、`maxQueueSize`、`maxWait`。请求级：`false`、`DiomanOfflineOptions`。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:99:- 模型：在线 → `next()`。离线：不排队或队列已满 → `ctx.fail(kind: offline, rea
son: …)`；排队 → `ctx.wait(恢复在线, timeout: maxWait)`，恢复后 `next()`，超时返回 `kind: offline, reason: 'timeout'`，
取消返回 cancel。位于 source 最内层，所以缓存命中在离线时照常返回。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:100:- 删除：`DiomanOfflineException`（改为 `kind: offline`）。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:101:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:102:## 6. envs
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:103:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:104:只有 `setup`，不进链：按 `rules` 选第一个命中的配置写进 `client.dio.options`
（写入前克隆，不与规则模板共享容器）。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:105:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:106:## 7. 内核服务
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:107:
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:108:- **key**：算法沿用 0.7（深模式递归排序 Map、List 保序、`ignores`、`namespace
`），由客户端级 `DiomanKeyOptions` 配置。请求级 `call.key`：省略为自动；字符串为显式 key；`false` 表示不参与 cache/shar
e。在 prepare 阶段之后惰性求值。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:109:- **取消**：`api.cancelAll(reason)` 返回本次中止的逻辑请求数；`api.pending`。
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:110:- **debug**：`Dioman(debug: true)` 使用默认日志器；记录调用点、插件告警（例如
请求体无法克隆、mock 缺少 `mockUrl`）。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:111:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:112:## 8. TokenManager
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:113:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:114:保留 `DiomanTokenManager` 接口（`accessToken`、`refreshToken`、`setTokens`、`
clear` 等），删除 `canRefresh`：是否已判过期的状态收进 auth 的实例内部。
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:115:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:116:## 8.1 B0 终审裁决（2026-10-29，补规格缺口）
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:117:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:118:B0 迁移清单标出 54 条存疑，归为下列规格缺口，按"现有功能能保留就保留"裁决：
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:119:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:120:| 缺口 | 裁决 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:121:|---|---|
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:122:| 请求级时钟覆盖（`DiomanAuthOptions.now`、`DiomanCacheOptions.now`） | 删除
；时钟统一由客户端 `DiomanClock` 注入。auth 的 `expiresAt`、`refreshLeeway` 请求级覆盖保留 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:123:| key 细粒度选项 | 客户端 `DiomanKeyOptions(fastMode, ignores, builder, names
pace)` 全部保留；请求级 `call.key` 接受 `String`、`false` 或 `DiomanKeyOptions`（ignores 与客户端取并集）；优先级：请求
级 builder > 请求级 key > 客户端 builder > 计算 |
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:124:| 插件状态与管理 API | 保留，经 `api.plugin<…Api>()` 取得：`DiomanBreakerApi`
（`stateOf`、`isOpen`、`bucketCount`）、`DiomanLoadingApi`（`activeCount`）、`DiomanOfflineApi`（`pending`、`flush`）、
`DiomanCacheApi`（`remove`、`clear`）；默认值常量（如 `DiomanBreaker.defaultIdleTtl`、`defaultMaxBuckets`）放在插件类上
。offline 在 dispose 时让排队请求以 `kind: offline, reason: disposed` 结束 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:125:| `onRetry` 抛错 | 归通知类：隔离并记日志，不影响请求结果 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:126:| 信封识别 | 沿用旧规则：Map 含 `code`，且含 `data` 或 `message` 之一即为信封
；工厂另提供 `isEnvelope(Map)` 钩子；请求级 `call.envelope: false` 表示本次按非信封处理（替代旧的 `shouldNormalize`） |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:127:| offline 队列满 | 沿用旧语义：挤出最早的请求（`reason: queueFull`）；`maxQue
ueSize` 为 0 表示不限，默认 50；`shouldQueue` 返回 false 的请求直接放行 |
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:128:| cache `cachePolicy.none`、`DiomanCacheOptions.maxEntries` 占位字段 | 删除：
cache 已按请求开启，`none` 多余，默认策略改为 memo；占位字段本来不被读取 |
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:129:| `DiomanSharePolicy.none` | 删除：关闭共享用请求级 `false` |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:130:| loading 请求级回调的触发、envs 合并 | 按新规格：按回调身份分开计数；envs 合
并时克隆容器 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:131:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:132:B2 终审补充：
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:133:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:134:| 缺口 | 裁决 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:135:|---|---|
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:136:| `BaseOptions` 分不清"省略"与"显式写了默认值" | envs 与 `extend` 共用"非空、
非默认字段覆盖"合并，三个 Map 容器深克隆后按顶层键合并。已继承的非默认值**不能**靠写默认值重置回去，此限制写进 `extend`
 契约；确有需要再加显式重置 API，不预做 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:137:| timeout 的真实连接计时 | 单测注入 `connectionTimeout` 只证明归类正确；真实
计时归 B7，按 testkit D22 结论 connect 档仍不可黑盒验证，G4 留作唯一缺口 |
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:138:| 旧套件首轮并发跑出两处 RangeError（loading_share/end、retry_share/race） | 
不归因 B2：B2 没碰旧源码与 `test_legacy/`，串行复验 580 全绿；旧套件在 B5 删除，不再投入 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:139:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:140:B3 终审补充：
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:141:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:142:| 缺口 | 裁决 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:143:|---|---|
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:144:| auth 的 `ready` 连续 3 次期间 token 都在变 | 不抛错（token 轮换是正常竞态，
不是使用者 bug）。与 axp 一致，以最后一次为准：注入最后一次 ready 的补丁，状态键记最后一次调用前的快照；之后若 401，默
认判定发现注入值与当前值不同，走 replay 自行纠正 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:145:| breaker 清桶 | `DiomanBreakerApi` 额外保留 `reset()`（旧版能力）；清表不取
消已放行的传输，旧窗口的结算不写进新桶 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:146:| 旧 `DiomanBreakerOpenException` 的 `retryableAt`、`bucketKey` | 失败载体仍
是 `kind: breaker, reason: 'open'`；两项信息改由 `DiomanBreakerApi.keyOf(request)` 与 `retryableAt(key)` 提供，只读，不
触发状态迁移 |
> docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:147:| 依赖 share/cache/mock 的 39 条组合迁移行 | B3 只验证自己这一侧的边界，真实
组合由 B4 承接，B4 报告逐行关闭 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:148:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:149:## 9. 行为变化汇总（写进 CHANGELOG 与迁移指南）
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:150:
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:151:| # | 变化 | 原因 |
  docs\design\pipeline-plugin-spec.md:152:|---|---|---|

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/b4_helpers.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/helpers.dart';

/// 回环成功响应。
const _body = '{"code":0,"data":"source"}';

/// 真实 Dio 的转发、共享取消与超时、缓存命中。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanMock.mockUrl × DiomanCache.option] 回环转发保留方法路径查询，回落后缓存零触网',
      () async {
    final server = await HttpServer.bind(InternetAddress.loopbackIPv4, 0);
    addTearDown(() => server.close(force: true));
    var probes = 0;
    var real = 0;
    server.listen((r) async {
      if (r.uri.path.startsWith('/mock')) {
        probes++;
        expect(r.uri.queryParameters['q'], 'value');
        r.response.statusCode = 404;
      } else {
        real++;
      }
      r.response.headers.contentType = ContentType.json;
      r.response.write(_body);
      await r.response.close();
    });
    final url = 'http://${server.address.address}:${server.port}';
    final dio = Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: url));
    addTearDown(() => dio.close(force: true));
    final api = Dioman<Result>(dio: dio, plugins: [
      DiomanMock<Result>(mockUrl: '$url/mock'),
      const DiomanCache<Result>()
    ]);
    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      expect(
          (await api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path,
                  query: {'q': 'value'}, call: b4Call(cache: true)))
              .ok,
          isTrue);
    }
    expect(probes, 2);
    expect(real, 1);
  });
  test('[DiomanShare.start] 回环首发取消及订阅者超时不终止共享 Dio', () async {
    for (final cancel in [true, false]) {
      final server = await HttpServer.bind(InternetAddress.loopbackIPv4, 0);
      final gate = Completer<void>();
      final arrived = Completer<void>();
      var hits = 0;
      server.listen((r) async {
        hits++;
        arrived.complete();
        await gate.future;
        r.response.headers.contentType = ContentType.json;
        r.response.write(_body);
        await r.response.close();
      });
      final dio = Dio(BaseOptions(
          baseUrl: 'http://${server.address.address}:${server.port}'));
      final api =
          Dioman<Result>(dio: dio, plugins: [const DiomanShare<Result>()]);
      final token = CancelToken();
      final a = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path,
          call: DiomanCall(
              key: b4Key,
              cancelToken: token,
              receiveTimeout: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
              sendTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 1),
              connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 2),
              options: {DiomanShare.option: true}));
      await arrived.future;
      final b = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path,
          call: DiomanCall(
              key: b4Key,
              receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 5),
              options: {DiomanShare.option: true}));
      await b4Turn();
      if (cancel) {
        token.cancel();
      }
      expect((await a).kind, cancel ? DiomanKind.cancel : DiomanKind.timeout);
      gate.complete();
      expect((await b).ok, isTrue);
      expect(hits, 1);
      expect(api.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
      expect(api.plugin<DiomanShareApi>()!.activeEntries, 0);
      api.dispose();
      dio.close(force: true);
      await server.close(force: true);
    }
  });
}
import 'constants.dart';
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'fake_clock.dart';
import 'fake_network.dart';

/// 测试共用结果类型。
typedef Result = DiomanResponse<dynamic>;

/// 测试中间件回调。
typedef Handler = Future<Result> Function(
    DiomanContext<Result>, DiomanNext<Result>);

/// 可观察 setup 和 dispose 的插件配置。
class TestPlugin extends DiomanPlugin<Result> {
  /// 创建测试插件。
  TestPlugin(this.name,
      {this.handler,
      this.stage = DiomanStage.attempt,
      this.enforce = DiomanEnforce.post,
      this.enabled = true,
      this.onSetup,
      this.onDispose,
      this.setupOnly = false});

  /// 唯一名称。
  @override
  final String name;

  /// 挂载阶段。
  @override
  final DiomanStage stage;

  /// 阶段内位置。
  @override
  final DiomanEnforce enforce;

  /// 开关。
  @override
  final bool enabled;

  /// 中间件。
  final Handler? handler;

  /// 安装观察器。
  final void Function()? onSetup;

  /// 释放观察器。
  final void Function()? onDispose;

  /// 是否只有 setup。
  final bool setupOnly;

  /// 安装次数。
  int setups = 0;

  /// 创建隔离状态实例。
  @override
  TestInstance? setup(Dioman<Result> client) {
    setups++;
    onSetup?.call();
    return setupOnly ? null : TestInstance(handler, onDispose);
  }
}

/// 独立测试实例，counter 可用于证明 extend 无共享状态。
class TestInstance extends DiomanPluginInstance<Result> {
  /// 保存回调。
  TestInstance(this.handler, this.onDispose);

  /// 执行处理器。
  final Handler? handler;

  /// 生命周期回调。
  final void Function()? onDispose;

  /// 当前实例执行数。
  int count = 0;

  /// 执行或直通。
  @override
  Future<Result> handle(DiomanContext<Result> ctx, DiomanNext<Result> next) {
    count++;
    return handler == null ? next() : handler!(ctx, next);
  }

  /// 释放本实例。
  @override
  void dispose() => onDispose?.call();
}

/// 创建使用假网络的客户端。
Dioman<Result> testClient(
        {FakeNetwork? network,
        List<DiomanPlugin<Result>> plugins = const [],
        DiomanResponseFactory<Result>? response,
        Object? debug,
        FakeClock? clock,
        Object Function(Result)? reject,
        DiomanKeyOptions key = const DiomanKeyOptions()}) =>
    Dioman<Result>(
        dio: Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: pipelineTestHttpsExampleTest))
          ..httpClientAdapter = network ?? FakeNetwork(),
        plugins: plugins,
        response: response,
        debug: debug,
        reject: reject,
        key: key,
        clock: clock ?? FakeClock());

/// 创建纯单元上下文，不执行客户端发送。
DiomanContext<Result> testContext(
        {CancelToken? token,
        FakeClock? clock,
        DiomanRequest? request,
        DiomanResponseFactory<Result>? factory,
        DiomanKeyOptions key = const DiomanKeyOptions(),
        DiomanLogger? log}) =>
    DiomanContext(
        id: 1,
        client: testClient(),
        request:
            request ?? DiomanRequest(RequestOptions(path: pipelineTestUnit)),
        state: DiomanContextState(token ?? CancelToken()),
        factory: factory ?? const DiomanResponseFactory<Result>(),
        keyService: DiomanKeyService(key),
        clock: clock ?? FakeClock(),
        log: log ?? (_) {});

Future<void> flush() async {
  for (var i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
    await Future<void>.value();
  }
}

### mock
- 选项：`enabled`（默认 true，装上即生效）、`routes`（`METHOD:path` → 处理函数，内联 mock）、`mockUrl`（转发到 mock 服务）、`fallbackWhen(R r)`，默认 `r.httpStatus == 404 || r.kind == network`。请求级：`false`、`true`、`DiomanMockOptions`。
- 模型：命中内联路由 → 用处理函数的结果经工厂造 `R`，直接返回。配了 `mockUrl` → `markReplayable()`，`probe = await next(patch: 改写到 mockUrl，并带"不走 cache、不参与 share"的状态标记)`；`fallbackWhen(probe)` 为真时再 `next()` 打真实接口。
- 变化：从传输层 adapter（2026-10-28）回到 source 阶段，`DiomanMockAdapter` 删除，cache 的 `x-dioman-mock` 规则删除。具备 testkit 的 `mock-in-chain` 能力。

### share
- 选项：`policy`（start、end、race、retry，默认 start）、`retries`、`interval`（retry 策略用）、`methods`（默认 GET、HEAD）。请求级（optIn）：`true`、策略枚举、`DiomanShareOptions`、`false`。`ctx.key()` 为 null 时直接放行。
- 模型：
  - start：首发 `next(patch: cancelToken = 共享 token，并去掉 Dio 层超时)`；每个订阅者用 `ctx.wait(共享结果, timeout: 自己的超时)`，各自离开只结束自己；最后一个订阅者离开时才取消共享请求。首发取消不连累跟随者。
  - end：每次都发，只有序号最新的结果落定。
  - race：每次都发，第一个 `r.ok` 的结果胜出，全部失败时用最后一个。
  - retry：共享同一个结果，失败时在内部按 `retries`、`interval` 重试，调用方只看到最终一次。
- 跟随者拿到同一个只读的 `R`。共享以"一次尝试"为单位；retry 在外层，每次重试都会重新进入 share。共享表按策略分开存放。
- 删除：结算登记簿、`hasMultipleDownstreamSettlers`、`registerDownstreamSettler`、`isJoined`。

### cache
- 选项沿用 0.7：`persist`（存储接口）、`cachePolicy`（memo、persist、both）、`expires`、`clone`、`maxEntries`、`shouldCache(request)`（默认 GET、HEAD）、`shouldStore(R r)`（默认 `r.ok`）、`clock`。请求级（optIn）：`true`、`Duration` = expires、`DiomanCacheOptions`、`false`。
- 模型：方法不允许、key 为 null、或带 mock 探测标记时直接放行；命中（惰性检查过期）直接返回，不触网；未命中时登记新鲜度屏障，`r = await next()`，屏障允许且 `shouldStore(r)` 才写入，`finally` 回收屏障记录。管理操作 `api.plugin<DiomanCacheApi>()` 提供 `remove`、`clear`；`clear()` 会作废在途的写入。
- 变化：`shouldStore` 默认就是 `r.ok`（归一化之后业务失败天然不会进缓存），不再需要和 normalize 接线。

### offline
- 选项沿用 0.7：`isOnline()`、`onConnectivityChanged`、`shouldQueue(request)`、`maxQueueSize`、`maxWait`。请求级：`false`、`DiomanOfflineOptions`。
- 模型：在线 → `next()`。离线：不排队或队列已满 → `ctx.fail(kind: offline, reason: …)`；排队 → `ctx.wait(恢复在线, timeout: maxWait)`，恢复后 `next()`，超时返回 `kind: offline, reason: 'timeout'`，取消返回 cancel。位于 source 最内层，所以缓存命中在离线时照常返回。
- 删除：`DiomanOfflineException`（改为 `kind: offline`）。

## 6. envs

只有 `setup`，不进链：按 `rules` 选第一个命中的配置写进 `client.dio.options`（写入前克隆，不与规则模板共享容器）。

## 7. 内核服务

- **key**：算法沿用 0.7（深模式递归排序 Map、List 保序、`ignores`、`namespace`），由客户端级 `DiomanKeyOptions` 配置。请求级 `call.key`：省略为自动；字符串为显式 key；`false` 表示不参与 cache/share。在 prepare 阶段之后惰性求值。
- **取消**：`api.cancelAll(reason)` 返回本次中止的逻辑请求数；`api.pending`。
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

/// 客户端和请求级的构键配置；空字段继承客户端值。
class DiomanKeyOptions {
  /// 创建配置，例如 `DiomanKeyOptions(fastMode: true)`。
  const DiomanKeyOptions(
      {this.fastMode, this.ignores, this.builder, this.namespace, this.key});

  /// 快速模式只编码方法和路径。
  final bool? fastMode;

  /// 与客户端取并集的忽略字段。
  final List<String>? ignores;

  /// 自定义构键器；抛错按程序异常处理。
  final String Function(RequestOptions)? builder;

  /// 客户端账号作用域，不应返回敏感令牌。
  final String? Function()? namespace;

  /// 请求级显式键；空串沿用自动计算。
  final String? key;
}

/// 与旧版算法独立共存的构键服务。
class DiomanKeyService {
  /// 创建服务，例如 `DiomanKeyService(const DiomanKeyOptions())`。
  const DiomanKeyService(this.options);

  /// 客户端构键配置。
  final DiomanKeyOptions options;

  /// 按请求 builder、请求 key、客户端 builder、计算的次序返回键；false 返回 null。
  String? build(RequestOptions request, [Object? override]) {
    if (override == false) return null;
    if (override != null &&
        override is! String &&
        override is! DiomanKeyOptions) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(override, 'key');
    }
    final o = override is DiomanKeyOptions ? override : null;
    final scope = options.namespace?.call();
    final explicit = override is String ? override : o?.key;
    if (o?.builder != null) return _scopedKey(o!.builder!(request), scope);
    if (explicit != null && explicit.isNotEmpty) {
      return _scopedKey(explicit, scope);
    }
    if (options.builder != null) {
      return _scopedKey(options.builder!(request), scope);
    }
    final ignores = _merge(options.ignores ?? const [], o?.ignores);
    return (o?.fastMode ?? options.fastMode ?? false)
        ? _scopedKey(_fastKey(request), scope)
        : _deepKey(request, ignores, scope);
  }

  /// 为快速键或自定义键增加独立作用域字段，空作用域保持原键。
  static String _scopedKey(String key, String? scope) =>
      scope == null || scope.isEmpty ? key : jsonEncode([key, scope]);

  /// Merges [override] into [base] (union), or returns [base] unchanged when
  /// [override] is `null`.
  ///
  /// 把[override]与[base]取并集合并；[override]为`null`时原样返回[base]。
  static List<String> _merge(List<String> base, List<String>? override) =>
      override == null ? base : {...base, ...override}.toList();

  /// 使用方法、来源和路径生成快速键。
  String _fastKey(RequestOptions o) =>
      '${o.method.toUpperCase()}:${o.uri.hasAuthority ? o.uri.origin : ''}${o.uri.path}';

  /// 分字段编码规范化查询及请求体，保留非结构化对象的身份。
  String _deepKey(RequestOptions o, List<String> ignoreKeys, String? scope) {
    final uri = o.uri;
    final params = <String, dynamic>{
      for (final e in uri.queryParametersAll.entries)
        if (!ignoreKeys.contains(e.key))
          e.key: e.value.length == 1 ? e.value.single : e.value,
    };
    final data = o.data;
    Object? body;
    if (data is Map) {
      final filtered = <String, dynamic>{
        for (final e in data.entries)
          if (!ignoreKeys.contains('${e.key}')) '${e.key}': e.value,
      };
      if (filtered.isNotEmpty) body = {'map': _encode(filtered)};
    } else if (data is String && data.isNotEmpty) {
      body = {'string': data};
    } else if (data != null) {
      body = {'identity': identityHashCode(data)};
    }
    return jsonEncode([
      _fastKey(o),
      _encode(params),
      body,
      if (scope != null && scope.isNotEmpty) scope,
    ]);
  }
  /// 写入闸门。
  Completer<void>? writeGate;

  /// 删除闸门，用于取消过期清理。
  Completer<void>? removeGate;

  /// 第一笔删除开始信号。
  final removeStarted = Completer<void>();

  /// 捕获读取时的快照，复现慢旧读取。
  bool snapshot = false;

  /// 可注入的操作异常。
  Object? error;

  /// 落盘次数。
  int writes = 0;

  /// 第一笔真实落盘开始信号，测试不猜测微任务轮数。
  final writeStarted = Completer<void>();

  /// 获取快照后可延迟返回。
  @override
  Future<dynamic> read(String key) async {
    final value = values[key];
    await readGate?.future;
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    return snapshot ? value : values[key];
  }

  /// 可阻塞写入。
  @override
  Future<void> write(String key, Map<String, dynamic> value) async {
    writes++;
    if (!writeStarted.isCompleted) writeStarted.complete();
    await writeGate?.future;
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    values[key] = value;
  }

  /// 删除条目。
  @override
  Future<void> remove(String key) async {
    if (!removeStarted.isCompleted) removeStarted.complete();
    await removeGate?.future;
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    values.remove(key);
  }

  /// 清空后端。
  @override
  Future<void> erase() async {
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    values.clear();
  }
}

/// 让 Dio 的异步转换与适配器完成当前事件轮，不推进真实计时。
Future<void> b4Turn() async {
  for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    await Future<void>(() {});
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

/// mock 探测期间绕过共享和缓存；只在当前 next 调用期间置位。
final sourceProbe = StateKey<bool>('mock probe');

/// 内联路由处理器；返回原始数据，由客户端工厂归一化。
typedef DiomanMockRoute = FutureOr<Object?> Function(DiomanRequest request);

/// 请求级 mock 配置；null 字段沿用插件配置。
class DiomanMockOptions {
  /// 例如 `DiomanMockOptions(mockUrl: 'https://mock.example')`。
  const DiomanMockOptions(
      {this.enabled, this.routes, this.mockUrl, this.fallbackWhen});

  /// 本次开关。
  final bool? enabled;

  /// METHOD:path 到处理器的映射。
  final Map<String, DiomanMockRoute>? routes;

  /// 探测服务地址。
  final String? mockUrl;

  /// 探测结果是否回落；抛错按程序错误处理。
  final bool Function(DiomanResponse<dynamic>)? fallbackWhen;
}

/// source 最外层 mock；内联命中直接经工厂返回，否则探测后按条件回落。
class DiomanMock<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> extends DiomanPlugin<R> {
  /// [routes] 优先于 [mockUrl]，例如 `DiomanMock(routes: {'GET:/pets': (_) => []})`。
  const DiomanMock(
      {this.enabled = true,
      this.routes = const {},
      this.mockUrl,
      this.fallbackWhen});

  /// 安装期开关，默认装上即生效。
  @override
  final bool enabled;

  /// 内联路由。
  final Map<String, DiomanMockRoute> routes;

  /// 转发服务地址。
  final String? mockUrl;

  /// 回落判定，默认 HTTP 404 或网络失败。
  final bool Function(DiomanResponse<dynamic>)? fallbackWhen;

  /// 稳定插件名。
  static const pluginName = 'dioman:mock';

  /// 请求级 bool 或配置键。
  static final option = DiomanOptionKey<DiomanMockOptions>(pluginName);

  /// 稳定名称。
  @override
  String get name => pluginName;

  /// 结果来源阶段。
  @override
  DiomanStage get stage => DiomanStage.source;

  /// 为客户端复制路由表。
  @override
  DiomanPluginInstance<R> setup(Dioman<R> client) => _Mock<R>(DiomanMockOptions(
      routes: Map.of(routes), mockUrl: mockUrl, fallbackWhen: fallbackWhen));
}

/// 客户端独立配置。
class _Mock<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> extends DiomanPluginInstance<R> {
  /// 保存预合并配置。
  _Mock(this.base);

  /// 插件配置。
  final DiomanMockOptions base;

  /// 探测状态只包围一次 next，回落恢复共享与缓存。
  @override
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> handle(
      DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next) async {
    final o = resolveOption<DiomanMockOptions>(
        ctx.request.call.options[DiomanMock.option],
        base: base,
        merge: (a, b) => DiomanMockOptions(
            enabled: b.enabled,
            routes: {...a.routes!, ...?b.routes},
            mockUrl: b.mockUrl ?? a.mockUrl,
            fallbackWhen: b.fallbackWhen ?? a.fallbackWhen));
    if (o == null || o.enabled == false) return next();
    final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
    if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
    final route = o.routes!['${ctx.request.method}:${ctx.request.uri.path}'];
    if (route != null) {
      final data = await ctx.wait(Future.sync(() => route(ctx.request)));
      return ctx.cancelled() ??
          ctx.factory.normalize(ctx.request,
              raw: Response<dynamic>(
                  requestOptions: ctx.request.toRequestOptions(),
                  statusCode: 200,
                  data: data));
    }
    final url = o.mockUrl;
    if (url == null || url.isEmpty) {
      ctx.log('[mock] 缺少 mockUrl，跳过探测');
      return next();
    }
    ctx.markReplayable();
    late DiomanResponse<dynamic> probe;
    ctx.set(sourceProbe, true);
    try {
      final path = ctx.request.uri.path;
      probe = await next(
          RequestPatch(path: '${url.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'/+$'), '')}$path'));
    } finally {
      ctx.set(sourceProbe, false);
    }
    final cancel = ctx.cancelled();
    if (cancel != null) return cancel;
    final fallback = o.fallbackWhen?.call(probe) ??
        (probe.httpStatus == 404 || probe.kind == DiomanKind.network);
    return fallback ? next() : probe;
  }
}
    } else if (strategy == DiomanClonePolicy.shallow) {
      copied = data is Map
          ? Map.of(data)
          : data is List
              ? List.of(data)
              : data;
    } else {
      throw ArgumentError.value(strategy, 'clone');
    }
    return ctx.factory.derive(r, copied);
  }

  /// 查缓存、登记屏障、只写入仍新鲜的结果，并在 finally 回收。
  @override
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> handle(
      DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next) async {

  /// 写入闸门。
  Completer<void>? writeGate;

  /// 删除闸门，用于取消过期清理。
  Completer<void>? removeGate;

  /// 第一笔删除开始信号。
  final removeStarted = Completer<void>();

  /// 捕获读取时的快照，复现慢旧读取。
  bool snapshot = false;

  /// 可注入的操作异常。
  Object? error;

  /// 落盘次数。
  int writes = 0;

  /// 第一笔真实落盘开始信号，测试不猜测微任务轮数。
  final writeStarted = Completer<void>();

  /// 获取快照后可延迟返回。
  @override
  Future<dynamic> read(String key) async {
    final value = values[key];
    await readGate?.future;
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    return snapshot ? value : values[key];
  }

  /// 可阻塞写入。
  @override
  Future<void> write(String key, Map<String, dynamic> value) async {
    writes++;
    if (!writeStarted.isCompleted) writeStarted.complete();
    await writeGate?.future;
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    values[key] = value;
  }

  /// 删除条目。
  @override
  Future<void> remove(String key) async {
    if (!removeStarted.isCompleted) removeStarted.complete();
    await removeGate?.future;
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    values.remove(key);
  }

  /// 清空后端。
  @override
  Future<void> erase() async {
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    values.clear();
  }
}

/// 让 Dio 的异步转换与适配器完成当前事件轮，不推进真实计时。
Future<void> b4Turn() async {
  for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    await Future<void>(() {});
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
class Store implements DiomanCachePersist {
  final values=<String,dynamic>{};
  dynamic read(String k)=>values[k];
  Future<void> write(String k,Map<String,dynamic> v)async{values[k]=v;}
  Future<void> remove(String k)async{values.remove(k);}
  Future<void> erase()async{values.clear();}
}

import '../support/pipeline/fake_network.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/helpers.dart';

/// 探测取消必须在回落判定前收尾。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanMock.fallbackWhen] 探测取消不回落不调用判定，内联异步取消消费迟到错误', () async {
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    final net = FakeNetwork()
      ..routes['GET:https://mock.test$b4Path'] = [
        FakeReply(status: 404, gate: gate.future)
      ];
    var decisions = 0;
    final api = testClient(network: net, plugins: [
      DiomanMock<Result>(
          mockUrl: 'https://mock.test',
          fallbackWhen: (_) {
            decisions++;
            return true;
          })
    ]);
    final pending = api.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    await b4Turn();
    api.cancelAll();
    expect((await pending).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    expect(decisions, 0);
    gate.complete();
    await b4Turn();
    expect(net.sends.length, 1);
    final inline = Completer<Object?>();
    final client = testClient(plugins: [
      DiomanMock<Result>(routes: {b4Route: (_) => inline.future})
    ]);
    final waiting = client.send<Object?>(b4Get, b4Path);
    await b4Turn();
    client.cancelAll();
    expect((await waiting).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    inline.completeError(StateError('late'));
    await b4Turn();
    expect(client.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
  });
}

codex
The source-plugin tests pass, but targeted real-HTTP checks reproduced lost mock query parameters and cached HTTP failures becoming successes. A separate lifecycle check reproduced an indefinite offline wait after plugin ejection.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Reject new queue entries after offline disposal — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\lib\src\pipeline\plugins\offline.dart:171-176
  If `eject(DiomanOffline.pluginName)` runs while a request is waiting in an earlier plugin, that request still follows its captured pipeline into this disposed instance. It then queues after `dispose()` has drained the queue and cancelled connectivity notifications. With the default unlimited `maxWait`, the request hangs indefinitely, even when connectivity returns. Check `disposed` before enqueueing and return the disposed failure immediately.

- [P2] Preserve embedded query parameters when forwarding mocks — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\lib\src\pipeline\plugins\mock.dart:120-122
  When a caller supplies query parameters in the path, such as `/pets?q=value`, `uri.path` drops them and the replacement path never restores them. These parameters are not present in the separate query map, so the mock receives `/mock/pets` without `q`, potentially returning a different result or incorrectly triggering fallback. Preserve the original URI query when constructing the probe URL.

- [P2] Preserve response classification across persistent cache hits — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\lib\src\pipeline\plugins\cache.dart:328-332
  With persistent caching and a custom `shouldStore` that accepts HTTP failures, the stored response is reconstructed as HTTP 200. For example, a cached 503 with a plain-text body returns `DiomanKind.http` initially but `DiomanKind.ok` on the next identical request, causing verb methods to return failed-response data instead of throwing. Persist and restore the response status/classification rather than unconditionally treating saved data as a successful response.
The source-plugin tests pass, but targeted real-HTTP checks reproduced lost mock query parameters and cached HTTP failures becoming successes. A separate lifecycle check reproduced an indefinite offline wait after plugin ejection.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Reject new queue entries after offline disposal — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\lib\src\pipeline\plugins\offline.dart:171-176
  If `eject(DiomanOffline.pluginName)` runs while a request is waiting in an earlier plugin, that request still follows its captured pipeline into this disposed instance. It then queues after `dispose()` has drained the queue and cancelled connectivity notifications. With the default unlimited `maxWait`, the request hangs indefinitely, even when connectivity returns. Check `disposed` before enqueueing and return the disposed failure immediately.

- [P2] Preserve embedded query parameters when forwarding mocks — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\lib\src\pipeline\plugins\mock.dart:120-122
  When a caller supplies query parameters in the path, such as `/pets?q=value`, `uri.path` drops them and the replacement path never restores them. These parameters are not present in the separate query map, so the mock receives `/mock/pets` without `q`, potentially returning a different result or incorrectly triggering fallback. Preserve the original URI query when constructing the probe URL.

- [P2] Preserve response classification across persistent cache hits — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\lib\src\pipeline\plugins\cache.dart:328-332
  With persistent caching and a custom `shouldStore` that accepts HTTP failures, the stored response is reconstructed as HTTP 200. For example, a cached 503 with a plain-text body returns `DiomanKind.http` initially but `DiomanKind.ok` on the next identical request, causing verb methods to return failed-response data instead of throwing. Persist and restore the response status/classification rather than unconditionally treating saved data as a successful response.
